# LogiCade fallback game inventory

Source: `data/manifest.json`. This inventory lists the 2,700 manifest entries, grouped by week, level, and difficulty. Each week-level has 10 Need Assistance, 10 Standard, and 10 Advance records (30 total). OpenCode variations are selected separately at runtime and are not additional fallback manifest entries.

Expected filename pattern: `game_week{week}_level{level}_difficulty({label})_{trial}.json`. Difficulty folder/label pairs: `needs_support` / `NeedAssistance`, `standard` / `Standard`, `advanced` / `Advance`.

## Local folder note

The local Week 1 / Level 1 / Standard folder contains 20 JSON files. The manifest lists the expected 10 Standard records (`game_0011`–`game_0020`, trials 1–10). The additional `_11`–`_20` files have `llm_w1_l1_standard_*` IDs and are not among the manifest entries, so the manifest-backed learner selection does not include them.
